`active_grad`: compute etc, weights. Derivative: g = Σ w_t * δ_t. Need efficient compute δ_t for selected t values.
 For each t (signed lag from idx-(n-1)):
 derivative wrt h_i:
 - if 0 <= i+t < n: add -x[i+t] (coefficient -1 * x[i+t])? Wait c_t = Σ h_{p+t}(1-h_p). Derivative wrt h_i:
   as upper: if i-t valid: + (1 - h[i-t])
   as lower: if i+t valid: + (-h[i+t])
 We can accumulate with slices:
   If i+t valid: indices p=i, term derivative = -x[i+t]. For i=0..n-1-t: grad[:n-t] += w*(-x[t:])? Because for i from0 to n-1-t, -x[i+t] => grad[0:n-t] += -x[t:].
   If i-t valid: indices i=t..n-1, derivative = 1 - x[i-t]. For i=t..n-1, grad[t:] += (1 - x[:n-t]).
 For t can be negative: conditions same with loops. We can general define for all t:
   k = t
   if k >=0:
      L=n-k
      grad[:L] += w * (-x[k:])   # i=0..L-1, i+k
      grad[k:] += w * (1.0 - x[:L]) # i=k..n-1, i-k
   else:
      k2=-k
      L=n-k2
      grad[k2:] += w * (-x[:L])? Let's map: i+k valid means i-k2 valid: i=k2..n-1, derivative -x[i-k2] => grad[k2:] += -x[:L].
      i-k valid: i-( -k2)=i+k2 valid: i=0..n-1-k2, derivative +1-x[i+k2] => grad[:L] += 1-x[k2:].
 So same but positive side terms swapped.
 We can unify:
   a = t
   if a >=0: lower_pos = :n-a? Actually use two segments:
      L=n-abs(a)
      if a>=0:
        neg = -x[a:]  # length L for i=0..L-1 (lower)
        pos = 1-x[:L]  # for i=a..n-1 (upper)
        grad[:L] += w*neg
        grad[a:] += w*pos
      else:
        b=-a
        neg = -x[:L]  # for i=b..n-1
        pos = 1-x[b:]  # for i=0..L-1
        grad[b:] += w*neg
        grad[:L] += w*pos

Pseudo:
```
def binary_sa(h,n,deadline,rng, iter_budget=...):
    x,y,c, ones, zeros, pos_o, pos_z = make_binary(...)
    maxc = int(c.max())
    best_cmax = maxc
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    T=... maybe based on maxc? Objective integer, temperature in units of maxc steps? Accept if random < exp(-delta/T). delta = new_max - best_max or current? Use current max for annealing? `cur_max`. If new_max < cur_max accept; if >, accept prob.
    For step in range(...):
       if time > deadline break
       pa=rng.randint(len(ones)); pb=rng.randint(len(zeros)); a=ones[pa]; b=zeros[pb]
       apply_swap(... a,b)
       nm = int(c.max())
       if nm < cur_max: accept
       elif nm > cur_max and rng.random() < exp(-(nm-cur_max)/T): accept
       else revert (apply_swap b,a)
       update accept lists if accepted
       if nm < best: save copy
       T *= 0.98 if no improvement for many steps? 
```

So delta for active k can be computed:
```
def swap_delta_k(a,b,k,x,y):
 delta=0
 j=b-k; if 0<=j<n: delta += y[j]
 j=a-k; if valid: delta -= y[j]
 j=b+k; if valid: delta -= x[j]
 j=a+k; if valid: delta += x[j]
 if k==0: delta -=2
 elif abs(k)==abs(a-b): delta +=1  # both ±? if k == b-a or a-b
 return delta
```
Since a,b are int. This is O(1).

Let's outline code:
```
import numpy as np
import time

def eval_c5(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full'))*dx)
```

Now weighted A computation:
```
def compute_A_from_W(x,y,W,n, nz):
    Ax = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)  # C_x
    Ay = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)  # C_y
    # w values length 2n-1 indexed off+k. But nz are indices p in W.
    for p in nz:
        w = W[p]
        if w == 0: continue
        k = p - (n-1)
        if k >= 0:
            L = n - k
            if L > 0:
                Ax[:L] += w * x[k:]
            # C_y[a]=Sum W_k y[a-k]. k>=0: a=k..n-1, y[a-k]=y[0:n-k]
            Ay[k:] += w * y[:L]
        else:
            b = -k
            L = n - b
            if L > 0:
                # x[a+k]=x[a-b], a=b..n-1
                Ax[b:] += w * x[:L]
                # y[a-k]=y[a+b], a=0..n-b-1
                Ay[:L] += w * y[b:]
    return Ax - Ay
```

```
def get_candidates(x,y,c,W,nz,ones,zeros,n,top_m=30):
    A = compute_A_from_W(...)
    off=n-1; w=W
    const=2.0*w[off]
    cand=[]
    # To get top m, we can for each a compute sb and if any < best skip? We'll keep list size limited using heap? Simpler: collect best 2 per a then sort.
    for a in ones:
        sb = A[a] - A[zeros] + w[(zeros - a) + off] + w[(a - zeros) + off] - const
        # sometimes zeros-a index out of range? diff min -(n-1), max n-1, +off 0..2n-2 yes.
        # use np.argpartition if len zeros> top_per
        if len(zeros) <= 10: idxs = np.argsort(sb)[:min(top_m, len(zeros))]
        else:
            idxs = np.argpartition(sb, min(5, len(zeros)))[:5]
        for idx in idxs:
            if sb[idx] < 0 or len(cand)<top_m: # collect
                b = zeros[idx]
                cand.append((float(sb[idx]), a, int(b)))
    cand.sort()
    return cand[:top_m], A
```

```
def active_fun(x, n, alpha, K, mu):
    c=np.correlate(x,1-x,mode='full')
    m=f float(c.max())
    if K < len(c):
       idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       ca=c[idx]
    else: ca=c
    am=ca.max()
    sh=alpha*(ca-am)
    sm=sh.max()
    z=np.exp(sh-sm).sum()
    return m + (sm+np.log(z))/alpha + mu*(x.sum()-n/2)**2
```

Grad:
```
def active_jac(x,n,alpha,K,mu):
  c=...
  if K<len: idx=argpartition...
  else idx=np.arange(len)
  ca=c[idx]; am=ca.max(); sh=alpha*(ca-am); sm=sh.max(); w=np.exp(sh-sm); w/=w.sum()
  g=np.zeros(n)
  for tidx,w in zip(idx,w.tolist()):
     k=tidx-(n-1); add_weighted_deriv(g, x, k, w)
  s=x.sum()-n/2; g += 2*mu*s
  return g
```
Need `add_weighted_deriv` top-level:
```
def add_deriv(g,x,k,wilt):
  if wilt==0: return
  if k>=0:
    L=n-k
    g[:L] += w*(-x[k:])
    g[k:] += w*(1.0-x[:L])
  else:
    b=-k
    L=n-b
    g[b:] += w*(-x[:L])
    g[:L] += w*(1.0-x[b:])
```

Thus calibration: We'll choose `flip_c = False` initially. Test apply_swap on copies:
```
def calibrate_orientation(x,y,c,n,a,b):
    # c is current np.correlate(x,y)
    for flip in (False, True):
       cs = c.copy() if not flip else c[::-1].copy()
       x_=x.copy(); y_=y.copy()
       apply_swap(cs,x_,y_,a,b,n)
       cdir = np.correlate(x_,y_,mode='full')
       if flip: cdir = cdir[::-1]
       if np.allclose(cs, cdir): return flip
    return None # fallback recompute
```

```
def calibrate_flip(x,y,c,n):
    a=int(np.flatnonzero(x)[0]); b=int(np.flatnonzero(y)[0])
    for flip in (0,1):
       cs = c.copy() if flip==0 else c[::-1].copy()
       xs=x.copy(); ys=y.copy()
       apply_swap(cs,xs,ys,a,b,n)
       cdir=np.correlate(xs,ys,mode='full')
       if flip: cdir = cdir[::-1]
       if np.allclose(cs,cdir): return flip
    return -1
```

```
def make_weights(c,n,tol=4):
   maxc=int(c.max())
   w=np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=np.float64)

   if np.any(mask):
       vals = (tol - gap[mask])  # peak tol, lower0

       # Optionally add small weight to lags c>=max-1? included
   return w, maxc
```

`active_subgradient(h,n,K,alpha)`:
```
c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
if K < len(c): idx=np.argpartition(c,-K)[-K:]
else: idx=np.arange(len(c))
ca=c[idx]
m=ca.max()
sh=alpha*(ca-m)
smax=sh.max()
exp_w=np.exp(sh-smax); w=exp_w/exp_w.sum()
g=np.zeros(n)
for ii,tt in enumerate(idx):
   pearl=float(w[ii]); if pearl<1e-12: continue
   add_deriv(g,h,tt-(n-1),pearl,n)
return g, c, m
```

Project_feasible implementation:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(h.copy(),0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(100):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
        if diff>0:
           room=1-h
           tot=room.sum()
           if tot<1e-15: break
           h += (diff/tot)*room
        else:
           mask=h>0
           tot=h[mask].sum()
           if tot<1e-15: break
           h[mask] += (diff/tot)*h[mask]
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final exact if n even? if not, can not exact integer but float ok.
    return h
```
For target fractional, iterative proportional scaling converges.

`fw_line_search(h,y,cur,n,deadline?)`: No deadline inside. We'll implement:
```
def fw_line_search(h, y, cur, n):
    ts = (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.15,0.2,0.3,0.4,0.5,0.7,1.0)
    best_t=0; best_v=cur
    for t in ts:
       ht = h + t*(y-h)
       v = float(np.max(np.correlate(ht,1-ht,mode='full')))
       if v < best_v:
          best_v=v; best_t=t
    if best_t > 0:
       # refine around
       lo = 0.0
       hi = 1.0
       # find bracketing from ts? easier sample around best_t ±0.08
       extra = np.linspace(max(0,best_t-0.2), min(1,best_t+0.2), 9)
       for t in extra:
          if t<=0: continue
          ht = h + t*(y-h)
          v=...
```

`fw_run`:
```
def fw_run(h,n,deadline,alpha_list=None):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')))
    if alpha_list is None: alpha_list=(100,500,2000)
    K = 128 if n<=600 else 256
    for alpha in alpha_list:
       no_impr=0
       while time.time()<deadline and no_impr<50:
          _,g = active_subgrad(h,n,K,alpha)  # also maybe c? We need cmax? active_subgrad computes c.
          if np.allclose(g,0): break
          y=linear_min(g,n)
          if not np.any(y != h): no_impr+=1; continue
          cur = float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')))  # avoid recompute? active returned cmax? We can return cmax.
          t,v=fw_line_search(h,y,cur,n)
          if t>0 and v+1e-12<cur:
             h = (1-t)*h + t*y
             h=project_feasible(h,n)
             if v<best_c: best_c=v; best_h=h.copy()
             no_impr=0
          else:
             no_impr+=1
    return best_h,best_c
```
But recomputes c in active_subgrad and cur again. Modify active_subgrad to return `g, cmax`. `cmax = float(c.max())`. Good.
However if y==h? y binary, h continuous rarely equal unless h exactly that binary at selected positions. Compare sum abs diff >1e-12.
Tie-breaking: If active_subgrad g all zeros, break. Could add random to g? Not needed.
Potential line search best_t may be small; no_impr if no sample improves. Could still improve with tiny t not sampled; but grad direction should reduce. Maybe use smaller t list including 0.005. Add `0.005,0.01`.

```
def state_copy(x,y,c,n):
    xc=x.copy(); yc=y.copy(); cc=c.copy()
    ones=np.flatnonzero(xc).astype(np.int32)
    zeros=np.flatnonzero(yc).astype(np.int32)
    po=np.full(n,-1,np.int32); pz=np.full(n,-1,np.int32)
    for i,a in enumerate(ones): po[a]=i
    for i,b in enumerate(zeros): pz[b]=i
    return xc,yc,cc,ones,zeros,po,pz
```

```
def make_weights(c,n):
    maxc=int(c.max())
    L=2*n-1
    w=np.zeros(L)
    gap=maxc-c  # int32
    tol = 3 if maxc > 80 else 4 ?
    mask = gap <= 4
    if not np.any(mask): return w,maxc
    m_idx=np.flatnonzero(mask)
    if m_idx.size > 80:
        # choose top 80 by c
        sel=np.argpartition(c[m_idx], -80)[-80:]
        m_idx=m_idx[sel]
    vals = maxc - c[m_idx]
    # If vals negative? no.
    # weight = (max_gap - vals +1)^3 where max_gap=4? For gap 0: (5)^3? Actually max_gap? Use (4 - vals)+1 =5 - gap; for gap4 weight1. peak5^3=125.
    ww = (5 - vals)
    ww = ww**3
    w[m_idx]=ww
    return w,maxc
```

`sa_step`: Need current max maybe curmax. It can accept equal with probability 1? Let's implement:
```
def sa_step(c,x,y,ones,zeros,po,pz,n,curmax,T,rng):
    a=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]; b=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
    apply_swap(c,x,y,a,b,n)
    nm=int(c.max())
    accept=False
    if nm < curmax: accept=True
    elif nm == curmax: accept = True  # or rng.random()<0.5
    elif T>1e-8 and rng.random() < math.exp(-(nm-curmax)/T): accept=True
    if accept:
       update_lists(...)
       return nm, True
    else:
       apply_swap(c,x,y,b,a,n)
       return curmax, False
```

```
def binary_refine(h,n,deadline,rng):
   x,y,c,ones,zeros,po,pz=make_binary_state(h,n,rng)
   curmax=int(c.max()); best_max=curmax
   bx,by,bc=state_copy(...)
   no_improve=0; sa_count=0
   while time.time()<deadline:
      # maybe if curmax==0 break
      improved,a,b=greedy_cycle(...)
      if improved:
          curmax=int(c.max())
          no_improve=0
          if curmax < best_max:
              best_max=curmax; bx,by,bc,ones,zeros,po,pz=state_copy(x,y,c,n)
          # continue
      else:
          no_improve += 1
      if time.time() > deadline: break
      if no_improve > 30:
          # do SA kick from best state (or current if improved recently)
          saved=state_copy(x,y,c,n)
          saved_max=curmax
          # start from best state to kick? If current not best, use best.
          x,y,c,ones,zeros,po,pz = bx.copy(),by.copy(),bc.copy(),ones_best... But state_copy best just returned lists; need copy to modify.
          kick_state=state_copy(bx,by,bc,n)
          curmax=int(kc.max()) # best_max
          T=1.0
          steps = 3000 if n<=600 else 1500
          for s in range(steps):
             if time.time()>deadline: break
             curmax, accepted = sa_step(... curmax,T,rng)
             if curmax < best_max:
                 best_max=curmax; bx,by,bc,...=state_copy(kx,ky,kc,n)
             # maybe every 500 decay T
             if s>0 and s%1000==0: T*=0.85
          # if no improvement, restore original current? We can set current to best state if improved else saved
          if best_max < saved_max:
              x,y,c,ones,zeros,po,pz=state_copy(bx,by,bc,n)
              curmax=best_max
              no_improve=0
          else:
              x,y,c,ones,zeros,po,pz=saved
              curmax=saved_max
              no_improve=0
          sa_count+=1
      # if no_improve huge and few sa, maybe restart from random state? optional.
   # At end, if best_max maybe from copies; ensure bx/by best current.
   return bx.astype(float), best_max
```

Now `run` orchestration:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-3
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float)
    except Exception:
       h0=None
    if h0 is None:
       n=500; x=rand? h0=0.5+...; project
    else:
       n0=len(h0)
    best_h=project_feasible(h0,n0); best_c=eval_c5(best_h,n0); best_n=n0
    # continuous FW on best_h for up to maybe 120s
    t_cont = min(time.time()+80, deadline-500)
    h_c,c_c=fw_run(best_h,best_n,t_cont)
    if c_c < best_c: update
    # prepare binary n
    n_bin = best_n if best_n%2==0 else best_n+1
    if time.time() < deadline-120:
       t_bin = min(time.time()+650, deadline-150)
       x_b,max_b = binary_refine(best_h, n_bin, t_bin, rng)
       c_b = max_b*2.0/n_bin
       if c_b < best_c: best_h=x_b; best_c=c_b; best_n=n_bin
    # If time, try other n (750,1000) using best_h
    for n_try in [750,1000]:
       if n_try%2: continue? 750 even yes
       if time.time() > deadline-100: break
       h_res=np.interp(...)
       t_try = min(time.time()+60, deadline-60)
       x_t,max_t=binary_refine(h_res,n_try,t_try,rng)
       c_t=max_t*2/n_try
       if c_t<best_c: update
    # final FW on best (up to 60s)
    if time.time() < deadline-10:
       h_f,c_f=fw_run(best_h,best_n,deadline-2)
       if c_f<best_c: update
    # final project, recompute
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=eval_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

```
import numpy as np
```

Pseudo code details:

```
def active_subgrad(h, n, K, alpha):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cmax = float(c.max())
    L = len(c)
    if K < L:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    else:
        idx = np.arange(L)
    ca = c[idx]
    am = ca.max()
    sh = alpha * (ca - am)
    smax = sh.max()
    ew = np.exp(sh - smax)
    w = ew / ew.sum()
    g = np.zeros(n)
    for j in range(len(idx)):
        wt = float(w[j])
        if wt < 1e-14: continue
        add_deriv(g, h, int(idx[j]) - (n - 1), wt, n)
    return g, cmax
```
In `fw_run`, K depends n. alpha list. If alpha large, sh negative maybe overflow? sh max 0, exp <=1. okay.

`add_deriv` must take n, not global.

`fw_line_search`:
```
def fw_line_search(h, y, cur, n):
    best_t=0.0; best_v=cur
    base=(0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7,1.0)
    for t in base:
       ht = h + t*(y-h)
       v=...
       if v < best_v - 1e-15:
          best_v=v; best_t=t
    if best_t > 0:
       lo=max(0.0,best_t-0.2)
       hi=min(1.0,best_t+0.2)
       extra=np.linspace(lo,hi,9)
       for t in extra:
          if t <= 0 or t > 1: continue
          ht=...
          v=...
          if v < best_v - 1e-15: ...
    return best_t,best_v
```
If best at t=1, extra lo 0.8 hi1.0; okay.

`fw_run`: after line search, if `t>0 and v < cur - 1e-12`; update. Recompute new_cmax to be safe:
```
h = h + t*(y-h); h=project_feasible(h,n)
newmax=float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')))
if newmax < cur - 1e-12:
   if newmax < best_cmax: best_cmax=newmax; best_h=h.copy()
   no_impr=0
else:
   no_impr += 1
```
If project changes, use newmax. Good.

`make_binary_state` returns flip. For `slow` path if flip<0, let's implement minimal:
```
def slow_binary_refine(x,y,c,ones,zeros,po,pz,n,deadline,rng):
    curmax=int(c.max()); best_max=curmax; bx=x.copy(); by=y.copy(); bc=c.copy()
    while time.time()<deadline:
       # 2000 random recompute swaps
       for _ in range(2000):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          a=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]; b=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
          x[a]=0; x[b]=1; y[a]=1; y[b]=0
          c[:] = np.correlate(x,y,mode='full').astype(np.int32)
          nm=int(c.max())
          if nm <= curmax:
             # accept? need update lists
             update_lists(...); curmax=nm
             if nm<best_max: update best
          else:
             x[b]=0; x[a]=1; y[b]=1; y[a]=0
             c[:] = np.correlate(x,y,'full').astype(...)
    return bx, best_max
```
But if orientation c not flipped? Slow uses c numpy; lists based on x. Good. This path too slow but backup. Need `update_lists` top-level. But in slow, if accept, lists updated; if reject, no. If curmax same accept; no temperature. This may improve. But if initial c orientation? def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
```
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+float(budget_s)-5.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0=np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        h0=None
    if h0 is None or len(h0)==0:
        n=500
        tv=np.linspace(0.0,4.0*np.pi,n,endpoint=False)
        h0=0.5+0.2*np.cos(tv)+0.08*np.cos(3.0*tv)
        h0=project_feasible(h0,n)
    n0=len(h0)
    best_h=project_feasible(h0,n0)
    best_c=eval_c5(best_h,n0)
    best_n=n0
    try:
       # first FW
       if time.time() < deadline-300:
           t_cont=min(time.time()+45.0, deadline-280.0)
           h_c,c_c=fw_run(best_h,best_n,t_cont)
           if c_c < best_c:
               best_c=c_c; best_h=h_c
       # binary
       if time.time() < deadline-80:
           n_bin=best_n if best_n%2==0 else best_n+1
           t_bin=min(time.time()+700.0, deadline-90.0)
           x_b, max_b = binary_refine(best_h, n_bin, t_bin, rng)
           c_b=float(max_b)*2.0/float(n_bin)
           if c_b < best_c:
               best_c=c_b; best_n=n_bin; best_h=x_b.astype(float)
       # maybe FW final
       if time.time() < deadline-8:
           t_f=deadline-2.0
           h_f,c_f=fw_run(best_h,best_n,t_f)
           if c_f < best_c:
               best_c=c_f; best_h=h_f
    except Exception:
       pass
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=eval_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```

Code draft:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def eval_c5(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*2.0/float(n))

def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy(),0.0,1.0)
    target=float(n)*0.5
    for _ in range(80):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-h
            tot=room.sum()
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h += (diff/tot)*room
        else:
            mask=h>0.0
            tot=h[mask].sum()
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h[mask] += (diff/tot)*h[mask]
    return np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
...
```
In project, if diff>0 and room sum zero but h all ones, impossible target half. okay.

`add_deriv`:
```
def add_deriv(g,x,k,wt,n):
    if wt == 0.0: return
    if k >= 0:
        L=n-k
        if L>0:
            g[:L] += wt * (-x[k:])
            g[k:] += wt * (1.0 - x[:L])
    else:
        b=-k; L=n-b
        if L>0:
            g[b:] += wt * (-x[:L])
            g[:L] += wt * (1.0 - x[b:])
```
Need `1.0 - x[b:]` length L? For k=-b, L=n-b. b<=n-1, b: length n-b =L. Good. For g[b:] length n-b = L. Good. For k>=0, g[k:] length n-k =L. Good.

```
kmin = int(np.floor(target))
if kmin >= n: kmin=n-1
y[order[:kmin]]=1
rem=target-kmin
```

`apply_swap`: ensure c int32 but works float too. If c float and x/y float? For binary int. Use slices:
```
def apply_swap(c,x,y,a,b,n):
    center=n-1
    yr=y[::-1]
    c[a:a+n] -= yr
    c[b:b+n] += yr
    c[center-b:center-b+n] -= x
    c[center-a:center-a+n] += x
    c[center] -= 2
    d=b-a
    c[center+d] += 1
    c[center-d] += 1
    x[a]=0; x[b]=1; y[a]=1; y[b]=0
```
`center-b+n` = n-1-b+n =2n-1-b; dimension okay. `center+d` int maybe np int? okay.
If c int32 and x int32, `c[center] -=2` works. If c flipped and orientation? yes.

`calibrate_flip`:
```
def calibrate_flip(x,y,c,n):
    ones=np.flatnonzero(x); zeros=np.flatnonzero(y)
    if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: return 0
    a=int(ones[0]); b=int(zeros[0])
    for flip in (0,1):
       cs = c.copy() if flip==0 else c[::-1].copy()
       xs=x.copy(); ys=y.copy()
       apply_swap(cs,xs,ys,a,b,n)
       cd=np.correlate(xs,ys,mode='full')
       if flip: cd=cd[::-1]
       if np.allclose(cs,cd,atol=1e-9): return flip
    return -1
```
If c is int32 and cd int64, allclose. Good.

`build_lists` maybe state_copy uses. We can just in state_copy.
```
def state_copy(x,y,c,n):
    xc=x.copy(); yc=y.copy(); cc=c.copy()
    ones=np.flatnonzero(xc).astype(np.int32)
    zeros=np.flatnonzero(yc).astype(np.int32)
    po=np.full(n,-1,dtype=np.int32); pz=np.full(n,-1,dtype=np.int32)
    po[ones]=np.arange(len(ones),dtype=np.int32)
    pz[zeros]=np.arange(len(zeros),dtype=np.int32)
    return xc,yc,cc,ones,zeros,po,pz
```
For c maybe int32. Good.

`update_lists` as above.

`make_binary_state`:
```
def make_binary_state(h,n):
    if len(h)!=n:
       xold=np.linspace(0,2,len(h),endpoint=False)
       xnew=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
       h=np.interp(xnew,xold,h)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    x=(h>=0.5).astype(np.int32)
    target=n//2
    cnt=int(x.sum())
    if cnt>target:
       idx=np.flatnonzero(x)
       rem=idx[np.argsort(h[idx])[:cnt-target]]
       x[rem]=0
    elif cnt<target:
       idx=np.flatnonzero(1-x)
       add=idx[np.argsort(-h[idx])[:target-cnt]]
       x[add]=1
    y=1-x
    c=np.correlate(x,y,mode='full').astype(np.int32)
    flip=calibrate_flip(x,y,c,n)
    if flip==1:
       c=c[::-1].copy()
    elif flip<0:
       flip=0
    xc,yc,cc,ones,zeros,po,pz=state_copy(x,y,c,n)
    return xc,yc,cc,ones,zeros,po,pz,flip
```
If flip<0, state_copy with c numpy orientation. Good.

`slow_binary_refine` if flip<0:
```
def slow_binary_refine(x,y,c,ones,zeros,po,pz,n,deadline,rng):
    curmax=int(c.max()); best_max=curmax
    bx,by,bc,_,_,_,_=state_copy(x,y,c,n)
    while time.time()<deadline-1:
       for _ in range(2000):
          if time.time()>deadline-1: break
          a=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]; b=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
          x[a]=0; x[b]=1; y[a]=1; y[b]=0
          c[:]=np.correlate(x,y,mode='full').astype(np.int32)
          nm=int(c.max())
          if nm <= curmax:
             update_lists(ones,zeros,po,pz,a,b)
             curmax=nm
             if nm<best_max:
                best_max=nm; bx,by,bc,_,_,_,_=state_copy(x,y,c,n)
          else:
             x[b]=0; x[a]=1; y[b]=1; y[a]=0
             c[:]=np.correlate(x,y,mode='full').astype(np.int32)
    return bx.astype(float), best_max
```
In reject, restore x: a was 1->0, b0->1; reject need b back0, a back1. Good. y restore. If accept, update lists. If equal max accept. This may drift but hill climb. Good.

`make_weights` as above.

`compute_A_from_W`.

`top_swap_candidates(A,W,ones,zeros,n)`:
```
def top_swap_candidates(A,W,ones,zeros,n):
    if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: return []
    off=n-1
    A1=A[ones]; A2=A[zeros]
    pz=zeros[None,:]-ones[:,None]
    D=W[off+pz]+W[off-pz]
    S=A1[:,None]-A2[None,:]+D-2.0*W[off]
    top=min(40,S.size)
    if top<=0: return []
    if top==S.size:
       flat=np.arange(S.size)[np.argsort(S.ravel())[:top]]
    else:
       flat=np.argpartition(S.ravel(), top-1)[:top]
    cols=len(zeros)
    cands=[]
    for fi in flat:
       r=fi//cols; ccol=fi%cols
       cands.append((float(S[r,ccol]), int(ones[r]), int(zeros[ccol])))
    cands.sort()
    return cands
```
Using argsort if top=S.size could be unnecessary but fine. For top maybe 40 and S size large. `S.ravel()` maybe copy; okay.
Potential memory: `pz` int32; `W[off+pz]` works. If S.size large and top=S.size (n small), argsort O(n log n) but n small.

`greedy_cycle`:
```
def greedy_cycle(c,x,y,ones,zeros,po,pz,n,test_limit=12):
    W,maxc=make_weights(c,n)
    nz=np.flatnonzero(W)
    if nz.size==0: return False,-1,-1
    A=compute_A_from_W(x,y,W,nz,n)
    cands=top_swap_candidates(A,W,ones,zeros,n)
    curS=float(np.dot(W,c))
    best_key=None; best_ab=None
    tested=0
    for sc,a,b in cands:
       if tested>=test_limit: break
       apply_swap(c,x,y,a,b,n)
       nm=int(c.max())
       if nm < maxc or nm == maxc:
           s=float(np.dot(W,c))
           key=(nm,s)
           if nm < maxc or (nm == maxc and s < curS - 1e-7):
               if best_key is None or key < best_key:
                   best_key=key; best_ab=(a,b)
       apply_swap(c,x,y,b,a,n)
       tested += 1
    if best_ab is not None:
       a,b=best_ab
       apply_swap(c,x,y,a,b,n)
       update_lists(ones,zeros,po,pz,a,b)
       return True,a,b
    return False,-1,-1
```
If `nm<maxc`, we accept even if key; comparing key tuple if nm<maxc all better than current; if two, lower s. Good. If `nm==maxc` and s<curS, key's s; comparing best_key with `(nm,s)`; if `nm` same, smaller s better. But if candidate `nm<maxc` and another `nm<maxc` lower, tuple works. If `nm` maxc and `s` lower but not <curS? we wouldn't consider. However if first candidate nm<maxc, best_key set; second candidate nm maxc but s much lower shouldn't replace because key=(maxc,s) > (oldmax-1, inf) tuple? Yes maxc > oldmax so not. Good.

`sa_step`:
```
def sa_step(c,x,y,ones,zeros,po,pz,n,curmax,T,rng):
    a=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]; b=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
    apply_swap(c,x,y,a,b,n)
    nm=int(c.max())
    accept=False
    if nm < curmax: accept=True
    elif nm == curmax: accept=True
    elif T>1e-8:
        accept = rng.random() < math.exp(-(nm-curmax)/T)
    if accept:
       update_lists(ones,zeros,po,pz,a,b)
       return nm,True
    apply_swap(c,x,y,b,a,n)
    return curmax,False
```
If T very large? okay.

`binary_refine`:

```
def binary_refine(h,n,deadline,rng):
    x,y,c,ones,zeros,po,pz,flip=make_binary_state(h,n)
    if flip<0:
       return slow_binary_refine(x,y,c,ones,zeros,po,pz,n,deadline,rng)
    curmax=int(c.max()); best_max=curmax
    bx,by,bc,bones,bzeros,bpo,bpz=state_copy(x,y,c,n)
    no_improve=0
    while time.time()<deadline-1:
       improved,a,b=greedy_cycle(c,x,y,ones,zeros,po,pz,n,12)
       if improved:
           curmax=int(c.max())
           no_improve=0
           if curmax < best_max:
               best_max=curmax
               bx,by,bc,bones,bzeros,bpo,bpz=state_copy(x,y,c,n)
       else:
           no_improve += 1
       if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
       if no_improve >= 20:
           # save current
           saved=state_copy(x,y,c,n)
           saved_max=curmax
           kx,ky,kc,kones,kzeros,kpo,kpz=state_copy(bx,by,bc,n)
           kmax=best_max
           T=1.0
           steps=5000 if n<=700 else 2500
           for step in range(steps):
              if time.time()>deadline-2: break
              kmax,acc=sa_step(kx,ky,kc,kones,kzeros,kpo,kpz,n,kmax,T,rng)
              if kmax < best_max:
                  best_max=kmax
                  bx,by,bc,bones,bzeros,bpo,bpz=state_copy(kx,ky,kc,n)
              if step>0 and step%2000==0:
                  T*=0.8
           if best_max < saved_max:
               x,y,c,ones,zeros,po,pz=state_copy(bx,by,bc,n)
               curmax=best_max
           else:
               x,y,c,ones,zeros,po,pz=saved
               curmax=saved_max
           no_improve=0
       # occasional resync to avoid drift
       if no_improve == 5:
           c2=np.correlate(x,y,mode='full').astype(np.int32)
           if flip: c2=c2[::-1]
           c[:]=c2
    return bx.astype(float), int(best_max)
```

```
 for _ in range(60):
  d=t-h.sum()
  if abs(d)<1e-12: break

   h += (d/s)*r if s>1e-14 else 0
  else:
   m=h>0; s=h[m].sum()

 return np.clip(h,0,1)
```

```
def active_subgrad(h,n,K,a):
 c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); m=float(c.max()); L=len(c)
 idx=np.argpartition(c,-K)[-K:] if K<L else np.arange(L)
 ca=c[idx]; mm=ca.max(); sh=a*(ca-mm); sm=sh.max(); w=np.exp(sh-sm); w/=w.sum(); g=np.zeros(n)
 for i,k in enumerate(idx):
  wt=float(w[i])
  if wt>1e-14: add_deriv(g,h,int(k)-(n-1),wt,n)
 return g,m
```

```
    h=h+t*(y-h)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def eval_c5(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, float)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * 2.0 / float(n))

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, float).copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    t = 0.5 * float(n)
    for _ in range(60):
        d = t - h.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-12:
            break
        if d > 0.0:
            r = 1.0 - h
            s = r.sum()
            h += (d / s) * r if s > 1e-14 else 0.0
        else:
            m = h > 0.0
            s = h[m].sum()
            if s > 1e-14:
                h[m] += (d / s) * h[m]
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

def add_deriv(g, x, k, wt, n):
    if wt == 0.0:
        return
    if k >= 0:
        L = n - k
        if L > 0:
            g[:L] += wt * (-x[k:])
            g[k:] += wt * (1.0 - x[:L])
    else:
        b = -k
        L = n - b
        if L > 0:
            g[b:] += wt * (-x[:L])
            g[:L] += wt * (1.0 - x[b:])

def active_subgrad(h, n, K, alpha):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cm = float(c.max())
    L = len(c)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:] if K < L else np.arange(L)
    ca = c[idx]
    am = float(ca.max())
    sh = alpha * (ca - am)
    sm = float(sh.max())
    w = np.exp(sh - sm)
    w /= w.sum()
    g = np.zeros(n)
    off = n - 1
    for i, kk in enumerate(idx):
        wt = float(w[i])
        if wt > 1e-14:
            add_deriv(g, h, int(kk) - off, wt, n)
    return g, cm

def linear_min(g, n):
    t = 0.5 * float(n)
    o = np.argsort(g)
    y = np.zeros(n)
    k = int(min(n - 1, t))
    y[o[:k]] = 1.0
    r = t - k
    if r > 1e-10 and k < n:
        y[o[k]] = r
    return y

def fw_line_search(h, y, cur, n):
    bt = 0.0
    bv = cur
    for t in (0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0):
        ht = h + t * (y - h)
        v = float(np.max(np.correlate(ht, 1.0 - ht, mode='full')))
        if v < bv:
            bv = v
            bt = t
    if bt > 0.0:
        lo = max(0.0, bt - 0.2)
        hi = min(1.0, bt + 0.2)
        for t in np.linspace(lo, hi, 8):
            if 0.0 < t <= 1.0:
                ht = h + t * (y - h)
                v = float(np.max(np.correlate(ht, 1.0 - ht, mode='full')))
                if v < bv:
                    bv = v
                    bt = t
    return bt, bv

def fw_run(h, n, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best = h.copy()
    bc = float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')))
    K = 128 if n <= 700 else 256
    for al in (200, 800, 2500):
        ni = 0
        while time.time() < deadline and ni < 35:
            g, cur = active_subgrad(h, n, K, al)
            y = linear_min(g, n)
            if float(np.abs(y - h).sum()) < 1e-13:
                ni += 1
                continue
            t, v = fw_line_search(h, y, cur, n)
            if t > 0.0 and v < cur - 1e-12:
                h = h + t * (y - h)
                nv = float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')))
                if nv < bc:
                    bc = nv
                    best = h.copy()
                ni = 0
            else:
                ni += 1
    return project_feasible(best, n), bc * (2.0 / float(n))

def apply_swap(c, x, y, a, b, n):
    ce = n - 1
    yr = y[::-1]
    c[a:a + n] -= yr
    c[b:b + n] += yr
    c[ce - b:ce - b + n] -= x
    c[ce - a:ce - a + n] += x
    c[ce] -= 2
    d = b - a
    c[ce + d] += 1
    c[ce - d] += 1
    x[a] = 0
    x[b] = 1
    y[a] = 1
    y[b] = 0

def calibrate_flip(x, y, c, n):
    ones = np.flatnonzero(x)
    zeros = np.flatnonzero(y)
    if len(ones) == 0 or len(zeros) == 0:
        return 0
    a = int(ones[0])
    b = int(zeros[0])
    for flip in (0, 1):
        cs = c.copy() if flip == 0 else c[::-1].copy()
        xs = x.copy()
        ys = y.copy()
        apply_swap(cs, xs, ys, a, b, n)
        cd = np.correlate(xs, ys, mode='full')
        if flip:
            cd = cd[::-1]
        if np.allclose(cs, cd, atol=1e-9):
            return flip
    return -1

def state_copy(x, y, c, n):
    xc = x.copy()
    yc = y.copy()
    cc = c.copy()
    ones = np.flatnonzero(xc).astype(np.int32)
    zeros = np.flatnonzero(yc).astype(np.int32)
    po = np.full(n, -1, dtype=np.int32)
    pz = np.full(n, -1, dtype=np.int32)
    po[ones] = np.arange(len(ones), dtype=np.int32)
    pz[zeros] = np.arange(len(zeros), dtype=np.int32)
    return xc, yc, cc, ones, zeros, po, pz

def update_lists(ones, zeros, po, pz, a, b):
    pa = po[a]
    pb = pz[b]
    ones[pa] = b
    po[b] = pa
    zeros[pb] = a
    pz[a] = pb
    po[a] = -1
    pz[b] = -1

def make_binary_state(h, n):
    if len(h) != n:
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(h), endpoint=False)
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
        h = np.interp(xn, xo, h)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    x = (h >= 0.5).astype(np.int32)
    target = n // 2
    cnt = int(x.sum())
    if cnt > target:
        idx = np.flatnonzero(x)
        x[idx[np.argsort(h[idx])[:cnt - target]]] = 0
    elif cnt < target:
        idx = np.flatnonzero(1 - x)
        x[idx[np.argsort(-h[idx])[:target - cnt]]] = 1
    y = 1 - x
    c = np.correlate(x, y, mode='full').astype(np.int32)
    flip = calibrate_flip(x, y, c, n)
    if flip == 1:
        c = c[::-1].copy()
    elif flip < 0:
        flip = 0
    return state_copy(x, y, c, n) + (flip,)

def make_weights(c, n):
    maxc = int(c.max())
    L = 2 * n - 1
    w = np.zeros(L)
    gap = maxc - c
    mask = gap <= 4
    if not np.any(mask):
        return w, maxc
    mi = np.flatnonzero(mask)
    if mi.size > 120:
        sel = np.argpartition(c[mi], -120)[-120:]
        mi = mi[sel]
    vals = maxc - c[mi]
    w[mi] = (5 - vals) ** 3
    return w, maxc

def compute_A_from_W(x, y, W, nz, n):
    Ax = np.zeros(n)
    Ay = np.zeros(n)
    off = n - 1
    for p in nz:
        wt = float(W[p])
        if wt == 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(p) - off
        if k >= 0:
            L = n - k
            if L > 0:
                Ax[:L] += wt * x[k:]
                Ay[k:] += wt * y[:L]
        else:
            b = -k
            L = n - b
            if L > 0:
                Ax[b:] += wt * x[:L]
                Ay[:L] += wt * y[b:]
    return Ax - Ay

def top_swap_candidates(A, W, ones, zeros, n):
    if len(ones) == 0 or len(zeros) == 0:
        return []
    off = n - 1
    A1 = A[ones]
    A2 = A[zeros]
    pz = zeros[None, :] - ones[:, None]
    S = A1[:, None] - A2[None, :] + W[off + pz] + W[off - pz] - 2.0 * W[off]
    top = min(40, S.size)
    if top <= 0:
        return []
    r = np.argpartition(S.ravel(), top - 1)[:top] if top < S.size else np.argsort(S.ravel())[:top]
    cols = len(zeros)
    out = []
    for fi in r:
        rr = fi // cols
        cc = fi % cols
        out.append((float(S[rr, cc]), int(ones[rr]), int(zeros[cc])))
    out.sort()
    return out

def greedy_cycle(c, x, y, ones, zeros, po, pz, n, test_limit=12):
    W, maxc = make_weights(c, n)
    nz = np.flatnonzero(W)
    if nz.size == 0:
        return False, -1, -1
    A = compute_A_from_W(x, y, W, nz, n)
    cands = top_swap_candidates(A, W, ones, zeros, n)
    curS = float(np.dot(W, c))
    best_key = None
    best_ab = None
    tested = 0
    for _, a, b in cands:
        if tested >= test_limit:
            break
        apply_swap(c, x, y, a, b, n)
        nm = int(c.max())
        if nm < maxc or nm == maxc:
            s = float(np.dot(W, c))
            if nm < maxc or (nm == maxc and s < curS - 1e-7):
                key = (nm, s)
                if best_key is None or key < best_key:
                    best_key = key
                    best_ab = (a, b)
        apply_swap(c, x, y, b, a, n)
        tested += 1
    if best_ab is not None:
        a, b = best_ab
        apply_swap(c, x, y, a, b, n)
        update_lists(ones, zeros, po, pz, a, b)
        return True, a, b
    return False, -1, -1

def sa_step(c, x, y, ones, zeros, po, pz, n, curmax, T, rng):
    a = ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
    b = zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
    apply_swap(c, x, y, a, b, n)
    nm = int(c.max())
    accept = False
    if nm < curmax:
        accept = True
    elif nm == curmax:
        accept = True
    elif T > 1e-8:
        accept = rng.random() < math.exp(-(nm - curmax) / T)
    if accept:
        update_lists(ones, zeros, po, pz, a, b)
        return nm, True
    apply_swap(c, x, y, b, a, n)
    return curmax, False

def binary_refine(h, n, deadline, rng):
    x, y, c, ones, zeros, po, pz, flip = make_binary_state(h, n)
    if flip < 0:
        return x.astype(float), int(c.max())
    curmax = int(c.max())
    best_max = curmax
    bx, by, bc, bones, bzeros, bpo, bpz = state_copy(x, y, c, n)
    no = 0
    while time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
        ok, a, b = greedy_cycle(c, x, y, ones, zeros, po, pz, n, 12)
        if ok:
            curmax = int(c.max())
            no = 0
            if curmax < best_max:
                best_max = curmax
                bx, by, bc, bones, bzeros, bpo, bpz = state_copy(x, y, c, n)
        else:
            no += 1
            if no == 10:
                c2 = np.correlate(x, y, mode='full').astype(np.int32)
                if flip:
                    c2 = c2[::-1]
                c[:] = c2
        if time.time() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        if no >= 20:
            saved = state_copy(x, y, c, n)
            smax = curmax
            kx, ky, kc, kones, kzeros, kpo, kpz = state_copy(bx, by, bc, n)
            km = best_max
            T = 1.0
            steps = 4000 if n <= 700 else 2000
            for st in range(steps):
                if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
                    break
                km, _ = sa_step(kx, ky, kc, kones, kzeros, kpo, kpz, n, km, T, rng)
                if km < best_max:
                    best_max = km
                    bx, by, bc, bones, bzeros, bpo, bpz = state_copy(kx, ky, kc, n)
                if st > 0 and st % 2000 == 0:
                    T *= 0.8
            if best_max < smax:
                x, y, c, ones, zeros, po, pz = state_copy(bx, by, bc, n)
                curmax = best_max
            else:
                x, y, c, ones, zeros, po, pz = saved
                curmax = smax
            no = 0
    return bx.astype(float), int(best_max)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    dl = t0 + float(budget_s) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, float)
    except Exception:
        h0 = None
    if h0 is None or len(h0) == 0:
        n = 500
        u = np.linspace(0.0, 4.0 * np.pi, n, endpoint=False)
        h0 = 0.5 + 0.2 * np.cos(u) + 0.08 * np.cos(3.0 * u)
    n0 = len(h0)
    best_h = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h, n0)
    best_n = n0
    try:
        if time.time() < dl - 300.0:
            h, c = fw_run(best_h, best_n, min(time.time() + 45.0, dl - 280.0))
            if c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_h = h
        if time.time() < dl - 80.0:
            nb = best_n + (best_n % 2)
            if nb > 1000:
                nb = 1000
            x, mx = binary_refine(best_h, nb, min(time.time() + 650.0, dl - 90.0), rng)
            cb = 2.0 * float(mx) / float(nb)
            if cb < best_c:
                best_c = cb
                best_n = nb
                best_h = x.astype(float)
        if time.time() < dl - 8.0:
            h, c = fw_run(best_h, best_n, dl - 2.0)
            if c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_h = h
    except Exception:
        pass
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
